Let's focus local optimizer.

Detailed local_search design:
Input: a (sum1, len n), time_limit.
State: current a, current score c_max etc. best a/score. We'll compute c each iter.
Parameters:
- peak_margin: initially 0.1 (active peaks within 10% of max), K_max maybe 10. Gradient weights top active.
- eta: base step for multiplicative, adapt from 0.5 to 10 maybe.
- random_kick_prob: increases over time/stuck.
- max consecutive failures -> random restart around current or best.

Loop until time limit:
1. Compute c = conv(current), m=max, score=2n*m. Update best if score < best_score (within 1e-7).
2. If time for periodic LP? Maybe every stuck 5000 or at start? We'll include a lightweight LP candidate if n<=512 and time remaining > 10s.
3. Choose active peaks:
   threshold = m * (0.9 + 0.05*rng?) maybe. active = c >= m - active_width*m. active_width start 0.05, expand if stuck? Limit top 8.
   If len active==0 -> [argmax]
   weights: use (c[active] - (m - width*m)) perhaps normalized; ensure positive. Or uniform for simplicity.
4. Compute s_unit = active_gradient(a, active, weights, n). Normalize by max(s)+eps.
5. Candidate generation:
   Generate 2-4 etas around current eta: [eta*0.5, eta, eta*2]. Maybe if eta too small/large.
   For each: cand = exp_update(a, s_unit, eta_try, floor=1e-12). score = 2n*max(conv(cand)). Choose lowest.
   If chosen_score < current_score - 1e-12:
       current=cand; current_score=chosen_score; accept; adjust eta: if best eta < current eta -> eta*=0.8; else eta*=1.1; clamp [0.05,20]. failures=0.
   Else:
       # try random perturb from current
       cand = random_perturb(current, temperature/sigma)
       score=...
       if score < current_score: accept if best else if Annealing probability.
       else maybe try finite step direction = -s_unit but additive? Or just reduce eta.
       failures++.
       if failures > threshold:
           # more aggressive: random restart from best with noise, or large multiplicative step with random peak.
           current = best + noise normalized; current_score=...
           fail reset, eta=1.

Need use simulated annealing to escape local min. Temperature schedule: initial temp 1e-4 maybe, cool. Accept worse if score - current < temp. Since score differences small (~1e-5). Use temp 1e-4 down to 1e-6. This can help. But we also need best separate.

Random perturb types:
- Gaussian multiplicative: a * exp(N(0, sigma)), sigma = 0.01 + 0.02*(failures/100). Normalize.
- Block: choose random block width 1-20, scale by exp(N(0,0.1)), maybe opposite side to balance.
- Symmetrize: (a + rev(a))/2 with noise.
- Peak targeted: reduce top contributors at active peaks randomly with larger step.
- Resample length? No.

- b=M*ones.
- c_obj=-ones(n).
- res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'disp':False, 'dual_obj_bound'?}). HiGHS may be slow. Wrap try. If not optimal g None.
- val = -res.fun. If val <= 1+1e-6 or not finite -> return None.

- Try blend alphas [0.1,0.2,0.5,0.8]: cand=(1-α)a+αh; normalize; score. Return best if < score(a).

```
def smooth_objective_theta(theta, tau, n):
    if DEADLINE is not None and time.monotonic() > DEADLINE:
       raise TimeLimit()
    m = np.max(theta)
    a = np.exp(theta-m); a/=a.sum()
    c = conv_fft(a)
    val, w = soft_max(c, tau)
    # h = correlate(w, a, valid)
    h = np.correlate(w, a, mode='valid')  # length n? Need verify if len(w)>=len(a)
    grad_a = 2.0*h
    dot = np.dot(a, grad_a)
    grad_theta = a*(grad_a - dot)
    return val, grad_theta
```

Thus implement FFT correlation for all n:
```
def correlate_valid_fft(w, v):
    nw=len(w); nv=len(v)
    size=1
    while size < nw: size <<=1  # need >=nw? maybe also nv? nw>=nv
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w,size)
    fv=np.fft.rfft(v,size)
    corr=np.fft.irfft(fw*np.conj(fv), size)
    return corr[:nw-nv+1]
```

Detailed `local_peak_search` possible code:
```
def local_peak_search(a0, wall_time, rng, verbose=True):
    a = normalize(a0)
    n=len(a)
    cur_score = 2*n*np.max(conv_fft(a))
    best=a.copy(); best_score=cur_score
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+wall_time
    eta=1.0
    fail=0
    width=0.05
    temp=1e-5? Score differences: target diff 0.01, but local diff maybe 1e-5. Use temp = 1e-4 maybe.
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1:
       c = conv_fft(a); m=np.max(c); score=2*n*m
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best=a.copy(); best_score=score; fail=0
       if it % 1000 ==0: print...
       # if stuck, increase width, temperature
       # choose active
       threshold = m - width*m
       active = np.where(c > threshold)[0]
       if len(active)>20:
           order = np.argsort(c[active])[-20:]
           active = active[order]
           w = c[active] - threshold # maybe
           if w.sum()<=0: w=np.ones(len)
       elif len(active)==0:
           active=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))]); w=np.array([1.0])
       else:
           w = c[active]-threshold; if w.sum()==0: w=ones
       s = active_grad(a, active, w, n)
       smax = s.max()
       if smax<=0: break
       s = s/smax
       # etas
       best_cand=None; best_cand_score=score
       etas=[]
       base=eta
       etas.append(base*0.3); etas.append(base*0.6); etas.append(base); etas.append(base*1.6)
       # Deduplicate sorted
       for e in etas:
          cand = exp_update(a, s, e, floor=1e-12)
          sc=2*n*np.max(conv_fft(cand))
          if sc < best_cand_score:
              best_cand_score=sc; best_cand=cand; chosen_e=e
       if best_cand is not None:
          # accept if improves or with prob temp
          improve = score - best_cand_score
          if improve > 1e-13 or (temp>0 and rng.random()<np.exp(-improve/temp)):
              a=best_cand; score=best_cand_score
              if score < best_score: best=...; fail=0
              # adapt eta
              if chosen_e < base: eta=max(0.05, base*0.8)
              elif chosen_e > base: eta=min(20, base*1.1)
          else:
              eta=max(0.05, base*0.7); fail+=1
       else:
          eta=max(0.05, base*0.7); fail+=1
       # periodic random kick
       if fail > 100 or rng.random()<0.02:
          cand = random_perturb(a, rng, 0.02+0.01*min(5,fail/50))
          sc = ...
          if sc < score or (temp>0 and accept): a=cand; score=sc; update best; fail=0
       # if fail too large, restart from best with noise
       if fail > 1000:
          a=random_perturb(best,...); score=...; fail=0; eta=1.0
       # cooling
       temp = max(1e-7, temp*0.9999)
       width = min(0.25, width*1.001) if fail>100 else max(0.02,width*0.999)
       it+=1
    # final local exact subgradient no line? maybe quick gradient descent
    return best,best_score, ...
```

LP implementation details with scipy.optimize.linprog. It may raise if no HiGHS. Wrap.
```
def lp_direction(a, max_M_factor=1.0):
   n=len(a); c=conv_fft(a); M=c.max()
   if M<=0: return None
   A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n), dtype=np.float64)
   for i in range(n):
      A[i:i+n,i]=a
   b=np.full(2*n-1, M)
   c_obj=-np.ones(n)
   try:
      res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'disp':False})
   except Exception:
      return None
   if res is None or not res.success:
      return None
   g=res.x; g=np.clip(g,0,None); S=g.sum()
   if S<=1+1e-9: return None
   h=g/S; return h
```
Need import linprog. If HiGHS not, linprog method='simplex' slower. Use default? We can pass method='highs'; if fails, maybe `method=None`. We'll try.

Now, smooth L-BFGS. Let's test gradient formula with fixed θ0. Need ensure soft value not multiplied by 2n. L-BFGS-B expects finite; if tau too small, soft max = max + τ log(count) + small. Weight distribution steep. h = correlate(w,a). gradθ. Could have NaNs if tau=0. Ensure tau>1e-12.

Top-level smooth function:
```
_SOFT_SIZE_CACHE = {}
def _rfft_size(m, n=None): ...
def smooth_conv_capacity(a): ...
def smooth_value_grad(a, fa, size, tau):
   c = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size)[:2*len(a)-1]
   cm = c - c.max()
   # stable weights
   z = cm / tau
   maxz=np.max(z) # 0
   ez=np.exp(z - maxz)
   w=ez/ez.sum()
   val = c.max() + tau * np.log(ez.sum())
   fw=np.fft.rfft(w, size)
   h=np.fft.irfft(fw*np.conj(fa), size)[:len(a)]
   grad_a = 2*h
   dot=np.dot(a, grad_a)
   grad_theta = a*(grad_a - dot)
   return val, grad_theta
```
Need c.max maybe. z max 0. Good.

But gradient with respect to u if θ0=0: yes partials. `value` unchanged by renormalization? a=softmax theta. Good.

`active_gradient(a, active, weights)`:
```
n=len(a); s=np.zeros(n)
for p,w in zip(active, weights):
    if w <=0: continue
    idx = int(p) - np.arange(n)
    valid=(idx>=0)&(idx<n)
    if np.any(valid): s[valid] += w * a[idx[valid]]
s[s<0]=0? weights positive a positive.

```
`select_active(c, width, kmax, rng)`:
```
m=np.max(c)
if m<=0: return [argmax],[1]
threshold = m*(1-width)
active=np.where(c >= threshold - 1e-15)[0]
if len(active)==0: active=np.array([argmax])
elif len(active)>kmax:
   take = np.argsort(c[active])[-kmax:]
   active=active[take]
   # maybe sort by c descending
   order=np.argsort(c[active])[::-1]; active=active[order]
# weights = max(c-active - threshold, small)
w = c[active] - threshold
if w.sum() <= 1e-15: w=np.ones_like(w)
else: w = w / w.sum()  # scale removed by later max but relative
return active,w
```
If width too large, includes many; kmax 12.
`random_perturb(a, rng, sigma, c=None)`:
```

if c is not None and rng.random()<0.4:
   # target top peak and reduce contributors, increase random elsewhere
   p=int(np.argmax(c)); idx=p-np.arange(n); valid...

   prod=np.zeros(n); prod[valid]=a[i]*a[idx]
   top_k=max(2, n//32)
   top_idx=np.argsort(prod)[-top_k:]
   cand[top_idx]*=np.exp(rng.normal(-0.05,0.02,top))
   others=np.setdiff1d(np.arange(n), top_idx)
   if len>0: inc=rng.choice(others, min(8,len), replace=False); cand[inc]*=np.exp(rng.normal(0.05,0.02,inc))
elif r<0.3: cand*=exp(normal(0,sigma,n))
elif r<0.6: block width etc
elif r<0.8: sym = 0.5*(a+rev); cand=sym*exp(normal(0,sigma/2,n))
```

```
def intensive_optimize(a0, wall_time, rng, tag):
   a=normalize(a0); cur=score; best...
   remaining=wall_time
   # local phase 1: use 50% if n<=512 else 35?

   update best
```

`local_peak_search` details:
We want not use enormous LP if time small. Add parameter `use_lp=False`, `lp_budget=0`.
```
def local_peak_search(a0, wall_time, rng, tag=''):
   a=normalize(a0); n=len(a)
   cur_score=score
   best=a.copy(); best_score=cur_score
   t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=min(_GLOBAL['deadline'], t0+wall_time)
   eta=0.8
   width=0.05
   temp=1e-5
   fail=0
   lp_count=0
   max_lp=2 if n<=384 and wall_time>300 else 0
   it=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1:
      if time.monotonic() > _GLOBAL['deadline']: break
      c=convolve_same(a); m=c.max(); score=2*n*m
      if score < best_score: best=a.copy(); best_score=score; fail=0
      # periodic print every 2000 or 30s? To avoid flood, every 5000 or 30s.
      if it % 2000 ==0: print tag...
      active,w = select_active(c, width, min(16, max(3,n//32)), rng)
      s=active_gradient(a, active, w)
      smax=s.max()
      if smax <=0: # random kick
         cand=random_perturb(...); score...
      else:
         s=s/smax
         # choose etas based on time left? 
         etas=[eta*0.4, eta, eta*1.8]
         dedup sorted; limit 3 (or 2 if n>768)
         best_cand_score=score; best_cand=None; chosen_alpha_eta=eta
         for e in etas:
             cand=exp_update(a,s,e)
             cm=np.max(convolve_same(cand)); sc=2*n*cm
             if sc < best_cand_score: ...
         if best_cand is not None:
             impr = score - best_cand_score
             if impr > 1e-14 or (temp>0 and rng.random() < np.exp(-impr/temp)):
                 a=best_cand; score=best_cand_score
                 if score < best_score: best=a.copy(); best_score=score; fail=0
                 # adapt eta
                 if chosen_eta < eta: eta=max(0.05, eta*0.85)
                 elif chosen_eta > eta*1.05: eta=min(5.0, eta*1.1)
                 else fail? no
             else:
                 eta=max(0.05, eta*0.8); fail+=1
         else:
             eta=max(0.05, eta*0.8); fail+=1
      # Random kick probability: if fail>0, p = min(0.2, 0.001*(fail)?)
      kick_prob = 0.0 + 0.0005*min(fail,200) if n<256 else 0.0002*min(fail,200)
      if fail>0 and rng.random()<kick_prob:
          cand=random_perturb(a, rng, sigma=min(0.2,0.02+0.01*min(fail,10)), c=c)
          sc=...
          if sc < score or (temp>0 and rng.random()<exp((score-sc)/temp)) : accept; fail=0 if improve
      # LP if fail > 1000 and lp_count<max_lp and time left>60:
      if fail>1000 and lp_count<max_lp and (deadline - time.monotonic())>60 and n<=512:
          h=lp_direction(a)
          lp_count+=1
          if h is not None:
             for alpha in [0.3,0.6]:
                cand=(1-alpha)*a+alpha*h; normalize
                sc=score
                if sc < score or accept: a=cand; score=sc; break
             if accepted: fail=0
      # reset if fail too high
      if fail > 5000:
          # restart from best with moderate noise
          sigma=0.05+0.05*rng.random()
          a=random_perturb(best, rng, sigma, c=None); score=score(a); fail=0; eta=1.0
      # cool temp
      temp=max(1e-9, temp*0.9998)
      # adapt width
      if fail>200: width=min(0.25, width*1.02)
      else: width=max(0.02, width*0.999)
      it+=1
   # final quick exact greedy? maybe do a few steps with larger candidate?
   return best,best_score
```
But note: In random kick acceptance, if worse but accepted, score becomes worse; best remains separate. Need update `score` variable. Good.

Maybe temp should be relative: 1e-4. We'll set temp initial = 1e-5 for n=256, maybe 1e-4? Score differences local could be 1e-6? If too low, no escape. Use temp=5e-5, cool to 1e-7. ```

```
def fourier_basis_matrix(n, M):
```

`_fft_size(m)`:
```
def _fft_size(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    if m in _FFT_SIZE_CACHE: return _FFT_SIZE_CACHE[m]
    size = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
    _FFT_SIZE_CACHE[m]=size
    return size
```
`normalize_sequence(a)`:
```
def normalize_sequence(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0: a=np.ones(1)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0/max(1,a.size))
    return a/s
```
If a.size=0 impossible.
`linear_convolution_same(a)`:
```
def linear_convolution_same(a):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    size=_fft_size(2*n-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size)
    return c[:2*n-1]
```
`score_normalized(a)`:
```
def score_normalized(a):
    a=normalize_sequence(a)
    n=a.size
    m=np.max(linear_convolution_same(a))
    if not np.isfinite(m): return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*m)
```
If n=0? return inf.
`exp_update(a, s, eta)`:
```
def exp_update(a, s, eta):
    s=np.asarray(s,float).ravel()
    if s.size != a.size: return normalize_sequence(a)
    eta=float(eta)
    if not np.isfinite(eta) or eta <=0: return normalize_sequence(a)
    # normalize s max to 1? caller may, but do anyway
    smax=np.max(s)
    if smax <= 1e-300: return normalize_sequence(a)
    s=s/smax
    s=np.clip(s,0.0,None)
    # Limit eta to avoid underflow? caller too.
    scale=np.exp(-eta*s)
    cand=a*scale
    tc=cand.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(tc) or tc <= 1e-300:
        cand=a.copy()
    else:
        cand=cand/tc
    # add tiny floor
    if cand.size > 0:
        floor=1e-15
        cand=np.maximum(cand, floor)
        cand=cand/cand.sum()
    return cand
```
If eta huge, exp underflow some to 0 but floor. If all underflow, cand=a. Good.

`active_gradient(a, active, weights)`:
```
def active_gradient(a, active, weights):
    n=len(a); s=np.zeros(n)
    idx_range=np.arange(n)
    for p,w in zip(active, weights):
        w=float(w)
        if not np.isfinite(w) or w <=0: continue
        p=int(p)
        idx=p - idx_range
        valid=(idx>=0)&(idx<n)
        if np.any(valid):
            s[valid] += w * a[idx[valid]]
    return s
```
Potential memory allocate idx each peak; K small ok. Could cache? no.

`select_active_peaks(c, width, kmax, rng)`:
```
def select_active_peaks(c, width, kmax, rng):
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if m <= 0 or not np.isfinite(m):
        p=int(np.argmax(c)); return np.array([p]), np.array([1.0])
    threshold=m*(1.0-width)
    if threshold < 0: threshold=0.0
    active=np.where(c >= threshold)[0]
    if active.size == 0:
        active=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
    else:
        if active.size > kmax:
            # choose top kmax by c, maybe random among tied? sort
            order=np.argsort(c[active])[-kmax:]
            active=active[order]
        # weights c - threshold; if all equal use 1
        w=c[active]-threshold
        # slight randomization to avoid exact determinism
        if rng is not None and w.size > 1:
            w = w * (1.0 + 0.05*rng.standard_normal(w.size))
        w=np.clip(w, 1e-15, None)
        sm=w.sum()
        if sm <= 0 or not finite: w=np.ones_like(w)
        else: w=w/sm
    return active, w
```
Adding noise to weights maybe helpful. Ensure finite.

`random_perturb(a, rng, sigma, c=None)`:
```
def random_perturb(a, rng, sigma=0.05, c=None):
    n=len(a); a=np.asarray(a,float); cand=a.copy()
    r=rng.random()
    if c is not None and r < 0.45:
        p=int(np.argmax(c))
        idx=p - np.arange(n)
        valid=(idx>=0)&(idx<n)
        prod=np.zeros(n)
        if np.any(valid):
            prod[valid]=a[valid]*a[idx[valid]]
        top_k=max(2, min(n//16, 64))
        top_idx=np.argsort(prod)[-top_k:]
        cand[top_idx] *= np.exp(rng.normal(-0.04, 0.02, size=top_k))
        others=np.setdiff1d(np.arange(n), top_idx)
        if others.size > 0:
            n_inc=min(max(2, n//32), others.size)
            inc_idx=rng.choice(others, size=n_inc, replace=False)
            cand[inc_idx] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.04, 0.02, size=n_inc))
    elif r < 0.7:
        # gaussian
        cand *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n))
    elif r < 0.85:
        # block
        width=max(1, int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n//8))))
        start=int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n-width)))
        sign=1 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1
        sign=sigma if sign>0 else -sigma
        cand[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(sign*0.5, sigma*0.5, size=width))
    else:
        # symmetrize with noise
        cand = 0.5*(cand + cand[::-1]) * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma*0.5, size=n))
    cand=np.clip(cand,0,1000)
    return normalize_sequence(cand)
```
Need if n=1 width etc. For n=1, top_k etc ok? `n//16=0`, max(2,0)=2 >n? `min(max(2,n//16),64)` gives 2 for n=1, argsort prod size1 top_k 2? `np.argsort(prod)[-2:]` returns array length1 okay? But top_k used in normal size=2 error. For n small, handle top_k = min(n, max(1,n//16)). Use top_k = min(n, max(1, n//16)); if top_k==0 set 1. Similar n_inc. Let's adjust.
```
top_k = min(n, max(1, n//16))
...
n_inc = min(others.size, max(1, n//32))
```
For n=1, width block: `width=max(1, int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n//8))))` if n//8=0 max2 => integers(1,2) ->1; start=int(rng.integers(0,max(1,n-width))) if n-width=0 -> max1 => integers(0,1) ->0. OK. `cand[start:start+width]` may only one.

```
def make_random_sequence(n, rng):
    method = int(rng.integers(0, 6))
    t=np.arange(n,dtype=float)/max(1,n-1)
    if method==0:
       a=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.5,size=n))
    elif method==1:
       phases=rng.uniform(0,2π,n)
       a=np.exp(0.3*rng.standard_normal(n) + 0.4*np.cos(2π*rng.uniform(0,10)*t + phases?))
```

```
    elif method==2:
       a=1 + rng.uniform(0,2)*(2*t-1) + rng.uniform(0,2)*(t-0.5)**2
       a=np.clip(a, 0.05, None)
    elif method==3:
       ph=rng.uniform(0,2π)
       alpha=rng.uniform(0.2,1.5)
       a=np.exp(alpha*np.cos(2π*rng.uniform(0.1,2.0)*t*t + ph) + rng.normal(0,0.1,n))
    elif method==4:
       # Fourier exp
       base=np.ones(n)
       for k in range(1, min(20, n//4)+1):
           if rng.random()<0.6:
               base += rng.uniform(0,1.5)*(1.0/k)*np.cos(2π*k*t+rng.uniform(0,2π))
               base += rng.uniform(0,1.5)*(1.0/k)*np.sin(2π*k*t+rng.uniform(0,2π))
       a=np.exp(base - base.mean() + rng.normal(0,0.05,n))
    else:
       # absolutely smooth random smoothed noise
       a=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.5,size=n))
       w=max(2, n//32)
       kernel=np.ones(w)/w
       a=np.convolve(a, kernel, mode='same')
```

`resize_sequence(a, n)`:
```
def resize_sequence(a, n):
    a=normalize_sequence(a)
    if a.size == n: return a
    if a.size <=1: return np.full(n, a.mean())
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,a.size)
    x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    b=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    return normalize_sequence(b + 1e-8)
```
Adding small positive.

`fourier_basis_matrix(n, M)`:
```
def fourier_basis_matrix(n, M):
    if n <=1:
       return np.ones((n,1))
    t=np.arange(n,dtype=float)/float(n) # 0.. <1
    cols=[np.ones(n)]
    cols.append((t-0.5))
    cols.append((t-0.5)**2)
    for k in range(1,M+1):
       w=2*np.pi*k*t
       cols.append(np.cos(w)); cols.append(np.sin(w))
    return np.column_stack(cols)
```
Need B immutable? objective uses. Good.

`fourier_objective(p, B)`:
```
def fourier_objective(p, B):
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)): return np.inf
    _check_deadline()
    theta=B.dot(p)
    theta=theta - np.max(theta)
    a=np.exp(theta)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <=0: return np.inf
    a=a/s
    n=a.size
    cm=np.max(linear_convolution_same(a))
    if not np.isfinite(cm): return np.inf
    f=float(2.0*n*cm)
    if f < _DE_STATE["best_f"]:
        _DE_STATE["best_f"]=f
        _DE_STATE["best_x"]=p.astype(float).copy()
    return f
```

```
def fourier_global_search(n, wall_time, rng):
    if differential_evolution is None or wall_time < 30: return None
    M = min(max(4, n//32), 16)
    B = fourier_basis_matrix(n, M)
    P = B.shape[1]
    bounds = [(-2.0,2.0)]*P
    bounds[0] = (-1.0,1.0)
    _DE_STATE = top-level; _DE_STATE["best_f"]=inf; best_x=None
    start=time.monotonic()
    local_deadline=min(_GLOBAL["deadline"], start+wall_time)
    _GLOBAL["deadline"]=local_deadline  # Is this problematic if called within other? It is nested in propose top-level? We set global to local. After function restore? Since sequential, but if we set inside, after returns need restore to original main deadline. We'll pass original, update, restore.
    try:
        res = differential_evolution(fourier_objective, bounds, args=(B,), maxiter=1000, popsize=12, tol=1e-5, init='latinhypercube', seed=int(rng.integers(0, 2**31-1)), callback=de_callback, updating='immediate')
        if res.x is not None: best_x = res.x
    except TimeLimitError:
        best_x = _DE_STATE["best_x"]
    except Exception:
        best_x = _DE_STATE["best_x"]
    finally:
        _GLOBAL["deadline"]=main_deadline
    if best_x is not None:
        theta=B.dot(best_x); a=exp... normalize
        score=score_normalized(a)
        return a,score
    return None
```

Now, LP direction:
```
def lp_direction(a, max_lsticks=3):
   if linprog is None: return None
   a=normalize_sequence(a); n=a.size
   if n > 600 or n < 2: return None
   c=linear_convolution_same(a); M=np.max(c)
   if M <= 1e-15: return None
   # Maybe M= max(M, 1e-12)
   A=np.zeros((2*n-1, n), dtype=np.float64)
   # build columns; for speed, use for i in range(n): A[i:i+n,i]=a
   for i in range(n):
       A[i:i+n, i] = a
   b=np.full(2*n-1, M, dtype=np.float64)
   c_obj=-np.ones(n)
   bounds=[(0.0, None)]*n
   try:
       res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'disp': False})
   except Exception:
       try:
           res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, options={'disp': False})
       except Exception:
           return None
   if res is None or not res.success: return None
   g=np.asarray(res.x,float)
   g=np.abs(g); S=g.sum()
   if not finite S or S <= 1.0 + 1e-8: return None
   return normalize_sequence(g)
```
If method 'highs' unavailable? scipy linprog default method='highs' in recent; if old, maybe not. Options? Could use method='interior-point'? We'll just pass method='highs'; if TypeError, fallback default. Python try.

```
def lp_polish(a, time_budget, max_calls=5):
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score_normalized(a); start=time.monotonic(); deadline=min(_GLOBAL..., start+time_budget)
    for _ in range(max_calls):
       if time > deadline-30: break
       h=lp_direction(a)
       if h is None: break
       for alpha in (0.2,0.5,0.8):
          cand=np.clip((1-alpha)a+alpha*h,0,None); cand=normalize(cand); sc=score
          if sc < best_score: best=cand; best_score=sc; a=cand; break
    return best,best_score
```

```
def should_accept(old, new, temp, rng):
```

Now, let's think about target. Suppose best after intensive remains 1.51. Could final code still fail. Can we make local search more aggressive to improve 1%? Let's maybe add a specialized "peak balancing" move using all active peaks and small steps. Multiplicative line search maybe enough. But let's design a deterministic active set Newton-like step.

At local optimum, active peaks have equal values; subgradients may not cancel. We can solve for a direction that reduces a weighted sum of active peaks with step optimized. Our multiplicative with weight average of active peaks is essentially steepest descent of weighted sum c_S. It may get stuck when active set changes. Smooth L-BFGS handles.

Could use `cvxpy` to solve for a small additive δ on simplex minimizing a quadratic approximation of active peaks? For current a and active set S with weights w, objective Q(δ)=∑_p w_p c_p(a+δ). Quadratic: Q = const + 2∑ w_p ∑ a_i δ_{p-i} + ∑ w_p ∑ δ_i δ_{p-i}. Constraints sum δ=0, maybe a+δ≥0, δ small. This is a trust region quadratic with nonconvex Hessian but maybe solve by gradient? Not.

Could use "water filling" on weights: For each active peak p, to reduce c_p to target T, roughly scale variables in pairs by reducing product. Multiplicative does.

Maybe use random coordinate ascent: pick two indices i,j and transfer mass; evaluate local effects. Could refine more than global multiplicative. Pair move: shift ε from i to j. Δc_k = ε(a_{k-j} - a_{k-i}) for valid (plus if i=j? quadratic small). This is like swapping mass. We can choose moves that reduce active peaks. But high dimension, search over pairs O(n^2); we can sample.

- Compute c, active peaks.
- For several random pairs (i,j), direction e_j - e_i (sum zero). Candidate a + ε(e_j - e_i). Choose ε by 1D? Impact on peak maybe linear. Could be costly.

```
"""
Hybrid search: paid...

import numpy as np
import time
import math
try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog, differential_evolution
except Exception:
    minimize=None; linprog=None; differential_evolution=None

_GLOBAL = {"deadline": float("inf")}
_FFT_SIZE_CACHE = {}
_SMOOTH_STATE = {"x": None}
_DE_STATE = {"best_f": float("inf"), "best_x": None}

class TimeLimitError(Exception):
    pass

def _check_deadline():
    if time.monotonic() > _GLOBAL["deadline"]:
        raise TimeLimitError()

def _fft_size(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    if m in _FFT_SIZE_CACHE: return _FFT_SIZE_CACHE[m]
    size = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
    _FFT_SIZE_CACHE[m]=size
    return size

...
```

`normalize_sequence`:
```
def normalize_sequence(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(arr.size, 1.0 / max(1, arr.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return arr / s
```
`math.isfinite` accepts float; if s nan. OK.

```
def linear_convolution_same(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0,float)
    size=_fft_size(2*n-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,size)
    out = out[:2*n-1]
    out[~np.isfinite(out)] = 0.0? Could hide issues. Maybe if nan return nan. But for search, if nonfinite return inf. We'll not modify? Use np.nan_to_num? If after roundoff -1e-16 okay. If nan, score inf. Keep.
    return out
```

`score_normalized`:
```
def score_normalized(a):
    a=normalize_sequence(a)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    c=linear_convolution_same(a)
    m=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else np.inf
    if not math.isfinite(m): return np.inf
    if m < 0 and m > -1e-12: m=0.0
    return float(2.0*n*m)
```

`metropolis_accept`:
```
def metropolis_accept(old_score, new_score, temperature, rng):
    if new_score <= old_score: return True
    delta = new_score - old_score
    if not math.isfinite(delta) or not math.isfinite(temperature) or temperature <= 0: return False
    if delta > 50.0 * temperature: return False
    return rng.random() < math.exp(-delta / temperature)
```
If delta/temp huge, exp underflow; use >50 maybe reject? We use 50 for probability 2e-22; okay. Could allow up to 1000? too small. Use 50.

`make_random_sequence`: Ensure normalize. We'll include more structured.
```
def make_random_sequence(n, rng):
    n=max(1,int(n))
    method = int(rng.integers(0, 8))
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=float)/float(max(1,n))
    if method == 0:
       a = np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.5,n))
    elif method == 1:
       alpha=float(rng.uniform(0.2,1.5)); ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
       a=np.exp(alpha*np.sin(2*np.pi*t + ph) + rng.normal(0,0.05,n))
    elif method == 2:
       a=1.0 + float(rng.uniform(0,2))*(2*t-1) + float(rng.uniform(0,2))*(t-0.5)**2
       a=np.clip(a,0.01,None)
    elif method == 3:
       alpha=float(rng.uniform(0.2,1.2)); ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)); beta=float(rng.uniform(0.1,3.0))
       a=np.exp(alpha*np.cos(2*np.pi*beta*t*t + ph) + rng.normal(0,0.05,n))
    elif method == 4:
       # multi tone
       base=np.ones(n)
       maxk=min(20,n//4)
       for k in range(1,maxk+1):
          if rng.random()<0.6:
             base += float(rng.uniform(0,1.0))* (1.0/k) * np.cos(2*np.pi*k*t + float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)))
             base += float(rng.uniform(0,1.0))* (1.0/k) * np.sin(2*np.pi*k*t + float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)))
       a=np.exp(base - base.mean())
    elif method == 5:
       # two-sided triangular
       p=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.9)); a=np.abs(t-p); a=np.exp(-float(rng.uniform(1,5))*a)
    elif method == 6:
       # smooth noise
       a=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.6,n))
       w=max(2, n//32)
       kernel=np.ones(w)/w
       a=np.convolve(a, kernel, mode='same')
    else:
       # random low Fourier with zero endpoints maybe
       base=np.sin(np.pi*t)
       amp=float(rng.uniform(0.2,1.5))
       freq=float(rng.uniform(0.5,5.0))
       a=base * np.exp(amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*t + float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)))) + 1e-6
    return normalize_sequence(a)
```
Need avoid variable `p` conflict no.
Method 5 `a` after np.exp boundaries; sum maybe.

`fourier_objective` and `de_callback`. In `fourier_objective`, `_check_deadline` may raise inside DE; good. But if global deadline later changes? no.
```
def fourier_objective(p, B):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=float)
    if p.size != B.shape[1] or not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
       return 1e100
    _check_deadline()
    theta=B.dot(p)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)): return 1e100
    theta=theta - np.max(theta)
    a=np.exp(theta)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300: return 1e100
    a=a/s
    n=a.size
    cmax=float(np.max(linear_convolution_same(a)))
    if not math.isfinite(cmax): return 1e100
    f=float(2.0*n*cmax)
    if f < _DE_STATE["best_f"]:
       _DE_STATE["best_f"]=f
       _DE_STATE["best_x"]=p.copy()
    return f
```
`de_callback(*args): _check_deadline()`

`fourier_global_search` with global main restore:
```
def fourier_global_search(n, wall_time, rng):
    if differential_evolution is None or wall_time < 30 or n < 16:
       return None, np.inf
    n=int(n)
    M=min(max(4, n//32), 16)
    B=fourier_basis_matrix(n,M)
    P=B.shape[1]
    bounds=[(-2.5,2.5) for _ in range(P)]
    if P>0: bounds[0]=(-1.0,1.0)
    # random init? DE init handles.
    old_deadline=_GLOBAL["deadline"]
    start=time.monotonic(); local_deadline=min(old_deadline, start+wall_time)
    _GLOBAL["deadline"]=local_deadline
    _DE_STATE["best_f"]=float("inf"); _DE_STATE["best_x"]=None
    best_x=None
    try:
       seed=int(rng.integers(0, 2**31-1))
       res=differential_evolution(fourier_objective, bounds, args=(B,), maxiter=2000, popsize=12, tol=1e-6, init='latinhypercube', seed=seed, callback=de_callback, updating='immediate')
       if res is not None and res.x is not None:
          best_x=np.asarray(res.x,float)
    except TimeLimitError:
       pass
    except Exception:
       pass
    finally:
       _GLOBAL["deadline"]=old_deadline
       if _DE_STATE["best_x"] is not None:
          best_x=np.asarray(_DE_STATE["best_x"],float)
    if best_x is None:
       return None, np.inf
    # reconstruct and maybe polish
    theta=B.dot(best_x); a=exp normalize
    sc=score_normalized(a)
    return a,sc
```

`softmax_fixed_from_u` top-level.
```
def softmax_fixed_from_u(u, n):
    u=np.asarray(u,float).ravel()
    if u.size != n-1: return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
       u=np.where(np.isfinite(u), u, 0.0)
    u=np.clip(u, -35.0, 35.0)
    theta=np.empty(n,float)
    theta[0]=0.0
    theta[1:]=u
    m=np.max(theta)
    a=np.exp(theta-m)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s<=1e-300: return None
    return a/s
```
If n=1, u.size 0, theta length1. Good.

`smooth_objective_from_u`:
```
def smooth_objective_from_u(u, tau, n):
    _check_deadline()
    u=np.asarray(u,float).ravel()
    if u.size != max(0,n-1):
       return 1e100, np.zeros(max(0,n-1))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
       u=np.where(np.isfinite(u),u,0.0)
    u=np.clip(u,-35,35)
    theta=np.empty(n); theta[0]=0; theta[1:]=u
    m=np.max(theta); a=np.exp(theta-m); s=a.sum(); if s<=... return 1e100,zeros
    a=a/s
    c,fa,size =? Need compute fa.
    size=_fft_size(2*n-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,size)[:2*n-1]
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)): return 1e100, zeros
    tau=float(tau)
    if not math.isfinite(tau) or tau <=1e-12:
       tau=1e-12
    cm=float(np.max(c))
    z=(c-cm)/tau
    ez=np.exp(z)
    sz=float(ez.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(sz) or sz <= 1e-300:
       w=np.zeros_like(c); imax=int(np.argmax(c)); w[imax]=1.0
    else:
       w=ez/sz
    val=cm + tau*math.log(float(sz)) if sz>0 else cm
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w,size)
    h=np.fft.irfft(fw*np.conj(fa), size)[:n]
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)): h=np.zeros(n)
    grad_a=2*h
    dot=float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad_theta=a*(grad_a - dot)
    grad_theta[~np.isfinite(grad_theta)] = 0.0
    return float(val), grad_theta[1:].astype(float)
```
Need if sz finite but log of huge? sz ≤ length(c) ~ 4096, log ok. For tau tiny, z differences maybe large negative; exp underflow for far; fine. If c-cm has tiny positive due roundoff, z maybe small positive >0, exp >1; max not exactly 0. That's okay. Could subtract np.max(z) for stability:
```
z=(c-cm)/tau
z -= np.max(z)
```
Then val = cm + tau*(np.max((c-cm)/tau)+log(sum exp(z))) = cm + tau*(0+log(sumexp original)+tau? if subtract max, need add. Simpler use `z=(c-cm)/tau; ez=np.exp(z)`; z max ≤? Some c may be >cm by roundoff? cm=max so no. If due cm float, max is max. So max 0. Good.

`smooth_minimize` more code. Need set old_deadline, main_deadline. In loop, after each stage restore old, but also set stage. Use:
```
def smooth_minimize(a0, wall_time, tag=''):
    old_deadline = _GLOBAL["deadline"]
    start=time.monotonic()
    main_deadline=min(old_deadline, start+wall_time)
    a=normalize_sequence(a0)
    n=a.size
    if n<=1 or minimize is None or wall_time < 20:
        _GLOBAL["deadline"]=old_deadline
        return a, score_normalized(a)
    _GLOBAL["deadline"]=main_deadline
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score_normalized(a)
    a_safe = a + 1e-12
    a_safe = a_safe/a_safe.sum()
    u=np.log(a_safe[1:] / max(a_safe[0],1e-300))
    # stages
    stages=[...]
    for idx, (factor, frac) in enumerate(stages):
        now=...; if now >= main_deadline - 2: break
        stage_deadline = min(main_deadline, now + max(2.0, frac*wall_time))
        cmax = best_score/(2.0*n) if best_score>0 else 0.003
        if not finite or cmax<=0: cmax=0.003
        tau = max(1e-6, factor*cmax)
        _GLOBAL["deadline"]=stage_deadline
        _SMOOTH_STATE["x"]=None
        try:
           res=minimize(smooth_objective_from_u, u.copy(), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=(tau,n), options={'maxiter':10000, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-9, 'maxcor':30}, callback=smooth_callback)
           if res is not None and res.x is not None:
               cand_u=np.asarray(res.x,float)
               if cand_u.size == n-1 and np.all(np.isfinite(cand_u)):
                   u=cand_u
        except Exception:
           pass
        finally:
           _GLOBAL["deadline"]=main_deadline  # restore for operations after? But next stage will set. If we restore main, then if we want stage time, good.
        if _SMOOTH_STATE["x"] is not None:
            cand_u=_SMOOTH_STATE["x"]
            if np.all(np.isfinite(cand_u)) and cand_u.size==n-1:
                u=cand_u
        a_new=softmax_fixed_from_u(u,n)
        if a_new is None:
            continue
        sc=score_normalized(a_new)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best=a_new.copy(); best_score=sc
        # track current a to continue; even if worse than best, use a_new? Could revert to best if much worse? For continuation, using current smoother maybe. Use a_new if finite, else previous.
        a=a_new
        if tag and idx%2==0: print...
    # restore global and final maybe local? return best,best_score
    _GLOBAL["deadline"]=old_deadline
    return best,best_score
```
But in finally we set to main_deadline, but after stage we want maybe not main until final. It's okay; main_deadline is larger than stage. But if we restore main, the next objective call before setting new stage? no. We set stage next. At the end restore old_deadline (original). Good.

```
def lp_polish(a0, wall_time, tag=''):
    old_deadline=_GLOBAL["deadline"]; start=time.monotonic(); main_deadline=min(old_deadline,start+wall_time)
    _GLOBAL["deadline"]=main_deadline
    a=normalize_sequence(a0); best=a.copy(); best_score=score_normalized(a); it=0
    while time.monotonic() < main_deadline - 30 and it<max_calls(5):
       h=lp_direction(a)
       if h is None: break
       improved=False
       for alpha in (0.2,0.5,0.8):
          cand=(1-alpha)*a + alpha*h; cand=normalize_sequence(cand)
          sc=score_normalized(cand)
          if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
              best=cand.copy(); best_score=sc; a=cand; improved=True; break
       if not improved:
          # maybe try alpha 1
       it+=1
    _GLOBAL["deadline"]=old_deadline
    return best,best_score
```

`local_peak_search`: large. Need use old_deadline main, set global? It doesn't set. Use old deadline to not interfere.
```
def local_peak_search(a0, wall_time, rng, tag='', candidate_count=3, use_lp=False):
    old_deadline=_GLOBAL["deadline"]; start=time.monotonic(); local_deadline=min(old_deadline, start+wall_time)
    a=normalize_sequence(a0); n=a.size
    if n <=0: return a, np.inf
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score_normalized(a)
    if local_deadline - start <= 1:
       _GLOBAL["deadline"]=old_deadline; return best,best_score
    _GLOBAL["deadline"]=local_deadline  # for any _check inside? We don't call _check much but okay.
    eta=0.8; width=0.05; temp=1e-5; fail=0; it=0
    kmax=max(2, min(16, n//32))
    lp_used=0
    lp_max=3 if (use_lp and n<=512 and wall_time>300) else 0
    last_print=start-30
    while True:
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now >= local_deadline - 0.2 or now >= _GLOBAL["deadline"] - 0.2: break
       c=linear_convolution_same(a)
       if c.size ==0: break
       m=float(np.max(c))
       if not math.isfinite(m): break
       score=2.0*n*m
       if math.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-14:
           best=a.copy(); best_score=score; fail=0
       if now - last_print > 20 or (it and it % 5000 ==0):
           print(f"{tag}: it={it} cur={score:.6f} best={best_score:.6f} eta={eta:.3f} width={width:.3f}")
           last_print=now
       active,w = select_active_peaks(c, width, kmax, rng)
       s=active_gradient(a, active, w)
       smax=float(np.max(s))
       cand_a=None
       if math.isfinite(smax) and smax > 0:
           s=s/smax
           etas=[]
           for mult in (0.3,0.6,1.0,1.6,2.5):
              e=eta*mult
              if 0.02 < e < 10.0:
                 etas.append(e)
           if not etas: etas=[0.5]
           etas=sorted(set([round(e,6) for e in etas]))[:candidate_count]
           cur_score=score
           best_cand=None; best_cand_score=cur_score; chosen_eta=eta
           for e in etas:
               cand=exp_update(a,s,e)
               cm=float(np.max(linear_convolution_same(cand)))
               sc=2*n*cm
               if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_cand_score:
                   best_cand_score=sc; best_cand=cand; chosen_eta=e
           if best_cand is not None:
               if metropolis_accept(cur_score, best_cand_score, temp, rng):
                   a=best_cand; score=best_cand_score
                   if math.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-14:
                       best=a.copy(); best_score=score; fail=0
                   else:
                       fail = min(fail+1, 100000) if not (best_cand_score <= cur_score) else 0
                   if chosen_eta < eta: eta=max(0.05, eta*0.85)
                   elif chosen_eta > eta: eta=min(8.0, eta*1.08)
               else:
                   eta=max(0.05, eta*0.85); fail+=1
           else:
               eta=max(0.05, eta*0.85); fail+=1
       else:
           fail += 1
       # random kick
       if fail > 0:
           kick_prob = 0.001 + min(0.15, 0.0004*fail) if n <= 768 else 0.0005 + min(0.1, 0.0002*fail)
       else: kick_prob=0.001
       if rng.random() < kick_prob:
           sigma = min(0.35, 0.02 + 0.01*min(20, fail//50))
           cand=random_perturb(a, rng, sigma, c)
           cm=float(np.max(linear_convolution_same(cand))); sc=2*n*cm
           if math.isfinite(sc) and metropolis_accept(score, sc, temp, rng):
               a=cand; score=sc
               if sc < best_score - 1e-14:
                   best=a.copy(); best_score=sc; fail=0
       # LP
       if use_lp and lp_used < lp_max and fail > 600 and (local_deadline - now)>120:
           h=lp_direction(a)
           lp_used +=1
           if h is not None:
              improved=False
              for alpha in (0.25,0.5):
                 cand=normalize_sequence((1-alpha)*a + alpha*h)
                 cm=float(np.max(linear_convolution_same(cand))); sc=2*n*cm
                 if math.isfinite(sc) and metropolis_accept(score, sc, temp, rng):
                    a=cand; score=sc
                    if sc < best_score - 1e-14: best=...; fail=0
                    improved=True
                    break
           if not (?): fail? 
       # reset if too stuck
       if fail > 4000:
           sigma = 0.05 + 0.1*rng.random()
           a=random_perturb(best, rng, sigma, None)
           score=score_normalized(a); fail=0; eta=1.0; width=0.08
       # adapt
       if fail > 250:
           width=min(0.30, width*1.02)
       else:
           width=max(0.02, width*0.9995)
       temp=max(1e-8, temp*0.9999)
       it +=1
    _GLOBAL["deadline"]=old_deadline
    return best, best_score
```
Need in LP if not improved, maybe fail++. Add.

```
def quick_optimize(a0, wall_time, rng, tag=''):
    a=normalize_sequence(a0); n=a.size
    if wall_time <= 2 or n <=0:
       return a, score_normalized(a)
    # If n large and time small, simple random/local
    cand_count=2 if n>1024 or wall_time<20 else 3
    a2,sc=local_peak_search(a, wall_time, rng, tag=tag, candidate_count=cand_count, use_lp=False)
    return a2,sc
```

```
def intensive_optimize(a0, wall_time, rng, tag=''):
    old_deadline=_GLOBAL["deadline"]
    start=time.monotonic(); main_deadline=min(old_deadline, start+wall_time)
    _GLOBAL["deadline"]=main_deadline
    a=normalize_sequence(a0); n=a.size
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score_normalized(a)
    # local1
    rem=max(0.0, main_deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 20:
       t_local = min(rem - 10, max(10, 0.5*wall_time))
       a,sc=local_peak_search(a, t_local, rng, tag=tag+'_lp1', candidate_count=4, use_lp=True)
       if sc<best_score: best=a; best_score=sc
    rem=...
    # smooth if n<=1200
    if rem > 60 and n <= 1200:
       t_smooth = min(rem*0.45, max(30, 0.25*wall_time), 600)
       try:
          a,sc=smooth_minimize(a, t_smooth, tag+'_sm')
          _GLOBAL["deadline"]=main_deadline
          if sc<best_score: best=a; best_score=sc
       except Exception:
          _GLOBAL["deadline"]=main_deadline

          if sc<best_score: ...

    # final local

    if rem > 20:
       a,sc=local_peak_search(a, rem-5, rng, tag+'_lp2', candidate_count=4, use_lp=False)
       if sc<best_score: ...
    _GLOBAL["deadline"]=old_deadline
    return best,best_score
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    try:
       total_budget=float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
       total_budget=2400.0
    if total_budget <= 0: total_budget=2400.0
    reserve = min(60.0, max(20.0, total_budget*0.025))
    main_deadline = t0 + max(60.0, total_budget - reserve)
    _GLOBAL["deadline"]=main_deadline
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_seq=None; best_score=float('inf')

    provided = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    if provided is not None:
       try:
          a=normalize_sequence(provided)
          sc=score_normalized(a)
          print(f"Provided: n={a.size}, score={sc:.6f}")
          if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
             best_score=sc; best_seq=a.copy()
       except Exception:
          pass
    if best_seq is None:
       a=normalize_sequence(np.ones(256))
       best_score=2.0; best_seq=a
       print("Fallback uniform 256")

    # Phase 1

    if total_budget > 120:
       print("Phase 1 intensive initial")

          a,sc=intensive_optimize(best_seq, min(phase1_time, main_deadline - time.monotonic() - 30), rng, 'init')

       except Exception:
          pass
    # Phase 2 exploration
    explore_deadline = main_deadline - max(120.0, 0.30*usable) # reserve final? Wait we need final after. Better set final_reserve = max(120, 0.30*usable)
```
