```python
"""
Hybrid search for non-negative step functions minimizing
2*n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)^2.

The program combines:
  * direct multiplicative peak-reduction updates driven by active
    convolution peaks,
  * simulated-annealing style perturbations,
  * linear-programming directions of the form max sum(g) s.t. a*g <= max(a*a),
  * smooth max convolution polynomial optimization via L-BFGS-B in log-space,
  * low-dimensional Fourier global searches via differential evolution,
  * multi-length exploration and progressive resizing.

All heavy diagnostics are handled internally; only propose_candidate is required.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog, differential_evolution
except Exception:
    minimize = None
    linprog = None
    differential_evolution = None


_GLOBAL = {"deadline": float("inf")}
_FFT_SIZE_CACHE = {}
_SMOOTH_STATE = {"x": None}
_DE_STATE = {"best_f": float("inf"), "best_x": None}


class TimeLimitError(Exception):
    pass


def _check_deadline():
    if time.monotonic() > _GLOBAL["deadline"]:
        raise TimeLimitError()


def _fft_size(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    if m in _FFT_SIZE_CACHE:
        return _FFT_SIZE_CACHE[m]
    size = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
    _FFT_SIZE_CACHE[m] = size
    return size


def normalize_sequence(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(arr.size, 1.0 / max(1, arr.size), dtype=np.float64)

    return arr / s


def linear_convolution_same(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    size = _fft_size(2 * n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)
    return out[: 2 * n - 1].astype(np.float64)


def score_normalized(a):
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")

    c = linear_convolution_same(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")

    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")

    if m < 0.0 and m > -1e-12:
        m = 0.0

    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def exp_update(a, s, eta):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    s = np.asarray(s, dtype=np.float64).ravel()

    if a.size == 0 or s.size != a.size:
        return normalize_sequence(a)

    eta = float(eta)
    if not math.isfinite(eta) or eta <= 0.0:
        return normalize_sequence(a)

    smax = float(np.max(s))
    if not math.isfinite(smax) or smax <= 1e-300:
        return normalize_sequence(a)

    s = s / smax
    s = np.clip(s, 0.0, None)

    scale = np.exp(-eta * s)
    cand = a * scale
    total = float(cand.sum())

    if not math.isfinite(total) or total <= 1e-300:
        cand = a.copy()
    else:
        cand = cand / total

    floor = 1e-15
    cand = np.maximum(cand, floor)
    cand = cand / cand.sum()
    return cand


def active_gradient(a, active, weights):
    n = a.size
    s = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    idx_range = np.arange(n)

    for p, w in zip(active, weights):
        w = float(w)
        if not math.isfinite(w) or w <= 0.0:
            continue

        p = int(p)
        idx = p - idx_range
        valid = (idx >= 0) & (idx < n)

        if np.any(valid):
            s[valid] += w * a[idx[valid]]

    return s


def select_active_peaks(c, width, kmax, rng):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.array([0]), np.array([1.0])

    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return np.array([0]), np.array([1.0])

    if m <= 0.0:
        p = int(np.argmax(c))
        return np.array([p]), np.array([1.0])

    threshold = m * (1.0 - float(width))
    if threshold < 0.0:
        threshold = 0.0

    active = np.where(c >= threshold)[0]

    if active.size == 0:
        active = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
    else:
        if active.size > kmax:
            order = np.argsort(c[active])
            active = active[order[-kmax:]]

        w = c[active] - threshold

        if rng is not None and w.size > 1:
            noise = 1.0 + 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(w.size)
            noise = np.clip(noise, 0.25, 1.75)
            w = w * noise

        w = np.clip(w, 1e-15, None)
        ws = float(w.sum())

        if not math.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
            w = np.ones_like(w)
        else:
            w = w / ws

    return active, w


def metropolis_accept(old_score, new_score, temperature, rng):
    if new_score <= old_score:
        return True

    delta = new_score - old_score
    if not math.isfinite(delta) or not math.isfinite(temperature) or temperature <= 0.0:
        return False

    if delta > 50.0 * temperature:
        return False

    return bool(rng.random() < math.exp(-delta / temperature))


def random_perturb(a, rng, sigma=0.05, c=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    cand = a.copy()
    r = float(rng.random())

    if c is not None and r < 0.45:
        c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
        if c.size > 0 and np.any(np.isfinite(c)):
            p = int(np.argmax(np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, -1.0)))
            idx = p - np.arange(n)
            valid = (idx >= 0) & (idx < n)

            prod = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            if np.any(valid):
                prod[valid] = a[valid] * a[idx[valid]]

            top_k = min(n, max(1, n // 16))
            if top_k > 0:
                top_idx = np.argsort(prod)[-top_k:]
                cand[top_idx] *= np.exp(rng.normal(-0.04, 0.02, size=top_k))

                others = np.setdiff1d(np.arange(n), top_idx)
                if others.size > 0:
                    n_inc = min(others.size, max(1, n // 32))
                    inc_idx = rng.choice(others, size=n_inc, replace=False)
                    cand[inc_idx] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.04, 0.02, size=n_inc))

    elif r < 0.70:
        sigma = float(sigma)
        if not math.isfinite(sigma) or sigma <= 0.0:
            sigma = 0.03
        cand *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n))

    elif r < 0.85:
        width = max(1, int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n // 8))))
        if width > n:
            width = n
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
        direction = 1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0
        sigma = float(sigma)
        if not math.isfinite(sigma) or sigma <= 0.0:
            sigma = 0.03

        cand[start : start + width] *= np.exp(
            rng.normal(direction * 0.4 * sigma, max(0.01, sigma * 0.5), size=width)
        )

    else:
        sigma = float(sigma)
        if not math.isfinite(sigma) or sigma <= 0.0:
            sigma = 0.03
        cand = 0.5 * (cand + cand[::-1])
        cand *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, max(0.01, sigma * 0.5), size=n))

    cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return normalize_sequence(cand)


def make_random_sequence(n, rng):
    n = max(1, int(n))
    method = int(rng.integers(0, 8))
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n))

    if method == 0:
        a = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, size=n))

    elif method == 1:
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.5))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        a = np.exp(alpha * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t + ph) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, size=n))

    elif method == 2:
        a = (
            1.0
            + float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0)) * (2.0 * t - 1.0)
            + float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0)) * (t - 0.5) ** 2
        )
        a = np.clip(a, 0.01, None)

    elif method == 3:
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.2))
        beta = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 3.0))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        a = np.exp(alpha * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * beta * t * t + ph) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, size=n))

    elif method == 4:
        base = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        maxk = min(20, max(1, n // 4))
        for k in range(1, maxk + 1):
            if rng.random() < 0.60:
                base += (
                    float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
                    * (1.0 / float(k))
                    * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)))
                )
                base += (
                    float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
                    * (1.0 / float(k))
                    * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)))
                )
        a = np.exp(base - base.mean())

    elif method == 5:
        center = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 0.9))
        phase = np.abs(t - center)
        a = np.exp(-float(rng.uniform(1.0, 5.0)) * phase)

    elif method == 6:
        a = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.6, size=n))
        w = max(2, n // 32)
        kernel = np.ones(w, dtype=np.float64) / float(w)
        a = np.convolve(a, kernel, mode="same")

    else:
        base = np.sin(np.pi * t)
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.5))
        freq = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 5.0))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        a = base * np.exp(amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * t + ph)) + 1e-6

    return normalize_sequence(a)


def resize_sequence(a, n):
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = max(1, int(n))

    if a.size == n:
        return a

    if a.size <= 1:
        return np.full(n, float(a.mean()), dtype=np.float64)

    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0) + 1e-8
    return normalize_sequence(b)


def fourier_basis_matrix(n, max_modes):
    n = max(1, int(n))
    max_modes = max(1, int(max_modes))

    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones((n, 1), dtype=np.float64)

    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    cols = [
        np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64),
        (t - 0.5),
        (t - 0.5) ** 2,
    ]

    for k in range(1, max_modes + 1):
        w = 2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * t
        cols.append(np.cos(w))
        cols.append(np.sin(w))

    return np.column_stack(cols).astype(np.float64)


def fourier_objective(params, basis):
    params = np.asarray(params, dtype=np.float64)

    if params.size != basis.shape[1] or not np.all(np.isfinite(params)):
        return 1e100

    _check_deadline()

    theta = basis.dot(params)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)):
        return 1e100

    theta = theta - float(np.max(theta))
    a = np.exp(theta)
    s = float(a.sum())

    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return 1e100

    a = a / s
    n = a.size
    cmax = float(np.max(linear_convolution_same(a)))

    if not math.isfinite(cmax):
        return 1e100

    val = float(2.0 * n * cmax)

    if val < _DE_STATE["best_f"]:
        _DE_STATE["best_f"] = val
        _DE_STATE["best_x"] = params.astype(np.float64).copy()

    return val


def de_callback(*args):
    _check_deadline()


def fourier_global_search(n, wall_time, rng):
    if differential_evolution is None or n < 16 or wall_time < 25:
        return None, float("inf")

    n = int(n)
    max_modes = min(max(4, n // 32), 16)
    basis = fourier_basis_matrix(n, max_modes)
    p = basis.shape[1]

    bounds = [(-4.0, 4.0) for _ in range(p)]
    if p > 0:
        bounds[0] = (-2.0, 2.0)

    old_deadline = _GLOBAL["deadline"]
    start = time.monotonic()
    local_deadline = min(old_deadline, start + float(wall_time))
    _GLOBAL["deadline"] = local_deadline

    _DE_STATE["best_f"] = float("inf")
    _DE_STATE["best_x"] = None

    best_x = None

    try:
        seed = int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1))
        res = differential_evolution(
            fourier_objective,
            bounds,
            args=(basis,),
            maxiter=1000,
            popsize=12,
            tol=1e-7,
            init="latinhypercube",
            seed=seed,
            callback=de_callback,
        )

        if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            best_x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)

    except TimeLimitError:
        pass
    except Exception:
        pass
    finally:
        _GLOBAL["deadline"] = old_deadline
        if _DE_STATE["best_x"] is not None:
            best_x = np.asarray(_DE_STATE["best_x"], dtype=np.float64)

    if best_x is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    theta = basis.dot(best_x)
    theta = theta - float(np.max(theta))
    a = np.exp(theta)
    a = a / a.sum()
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    return a, score_normalized(a)


def softmax_fixed_from_u(u, n):
    n = int(n)
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64).ravel()

    if u.size != max(0, n - 1):
        return None

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
        u = np.where(np.isfinite(u), u, 0.0)

    u = np.clip(u, -35.0, 35.0)

    theta = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    theta[0] = 0.0
    if n > 1:
        theta[1:] = u

    shift = float(np.max(theta))
    a = np.exp(theta - shift)
    s = float(a.sum())

    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None

    return a / s


def smooth_objective_from_u(u, tau, n):
    _check_deadline()

    n = int(n)
    dim = max(0, n - 1)

    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if u.size != dim:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float64)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
        u = np.where(np.isfinite(u), u, 0.0)

    u = np.clip(u, -35.0, 35.0)

    theta = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    theta[0] = 0.0
    if n > 1:
        theta[1:] = u

    shift = float(np.max(theta))
    a = np.exp(theta - shift)
    s = float(a.sum())

    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float64)

    a = a / s

    size = _fft_size(2 * n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)[: 2 * n - 1].astype(np.float64)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float64)

    tau = float(tau)
    if not math.isfinite(tau) or tau <= 1e-12:
        tau = 1e-12

    cm = float(np.max(c))
    z = (c - cm) / tau

    ez = np.exp(z)
    sz = float(ez.sum())

    if not math.isfinite(sz) or sz <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = ez / sz

    val = cm + tau * math.log(float(sz)) if sz > 0.0 else cm

    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    h = np.fft.irfft(fw * np.conj(fa), size)[:n].astype(np.float64)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    grad_a = 2.0 * h
    dot = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad_theta = a * (grad_a - dot)
    grad_theta = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_theta), grad_theta, 0.0)

    if dim == 0:
        return float(val), np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    return float(val), grad_theta[1:].astype(np.float64)


def smooth_callback(xk):
    _SMOOTH_STATE["x"] = np.asarray(xk, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    _check_deadline()


def smooth_minimize(a0, wall_time, tag=""):
    old_deadline = _GLOBAL["deadline"]
    start = time.monotonic()
    main_deadline = min(old_deadline, start + float(wall_time))

    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    n = a.size

    if minimize is None or n <= 1 or wall_time < 20:
        _GLOBAL["deadline"] = old_deadline
        return a, score_normalized(a)

    _GLOBAL["deadline"] = main_deadline

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = score_normalized(a)

    a_safe = a + 1e-12
    a_safe = a_safe / a_safe.sum()
    u = np.log(a_safe[1:] / float(max(a_safe[0], 1e-300)))
    u = np.where(np.isfinite(u), u, 0.0)
    u = np.clip(u, -35.0, 35.0)

    stages = [
        (1.0, 0.05),
        (0.5, 0.07),
        (0.25, 0.09),
        (0.12, 0.11),
        (0.06, 0.12),
        (0.03, 0.12),
        (0.015, 0.14),
        (0.008, 0.14),
        (0.004, 0.16),
    ]

    try:
        for idx, (factor, frac) in enumerate(stages):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now >= main_deadline - 2.0:
                break

            stage_deadline = min(main_deadline, now + max(2.0, float(frac) * float(wall_time)))

            cmax = best_score / (2.0 * n) if best_score > 0.0 else 0.003
            if not math.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
                cmax = 0.003

            tau = max(1e-6, float(factor) * cmax)

            _GLOBAL["deadline"] = stage_deadline
            _SMOOTH_STATE["x"] = None

            try:
                res = minimize(
                    smooth_objective_from_u,
                    u.copy(),
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    jac=True,
                    args=(tau, n),
                    options={
                        "maxiter": 10000,
                        "ftol": 1e-12,
                        "gtol": 1e-9,
                        "maxcor": 30,
                    },
                    callback=smooth_callback,
                )

                if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
                    cand_u = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
                    if cand_u.size == n - 1 and np.all(np.isfinite(cand_u)):
                        u = np.clip(cand_u, -35.0, 35.0)

            except Exception:
                pass
            finally:
                _GLOBAL["deadline"] = main_deadline

            if _SMOOTH_STATE["x"] is not None:
                cand_u = np.asarray(_SMOOTH_STATE["x"], dtype=np.float64)
                if cand_u.size == n - 1 and np.all(np.isfinite(cand_u)):
                    u = np.clip(cand_u, -35.0, 35.0)

            a_new = softmax_fixed_from_u(u, n)
            if a_new is None:
                continue

            sc = score_normalized(a_new)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best = a_new.copy()
                best_score = sc

            a = a_new

            if tag and (idx % 2 == 0 or time.monotonic() - start > 20.0):
                print(f"{tag}: smooth stage {idx} tau={tau:.3e} best={best_score:.6f}")

    finally:
        _GLOBAL["deadline"] = old_deadline

    return best, best_score


def lp_direction(a):
    if linprog is None:
        return None

    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = a.size

    if n < 2 or n > 700:
        return None

    c = linear_convolution_same(a)
    m = float(np.max(c))

    if not math.isfinite(m) or m <= 1e-15:
        return None

    A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(n):
        A[i : i + n, i] = a

    b = np.full(2 * n - 1, m, dtype=np.float64)
    obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None) for _ in range(n)]

    res = None

    try:
        res = linprog(obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method="highs", options={"disp": False})
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, options={"disp": False})
        except Exception:
            res = None

    if res is None or not bool(getattr(res, "success", False)):
        return None

    g = np.asarray(getattr(res, "x", None), dtype=np.float64)
    if g is None or g.size != n:
        return None

    g = np.abs(g)
    s = float(g.sum())

    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1.0 + 1e-8:
        return None

    return normalize_sequence(g)


def lp_polish(a0, wall_time, tag=""):
    old_deadline = _GLOBAL["deadline"]
    start = time.monotonic()
    main_deadline = min(old_deadline, start + float(wall_time))

    if main_deadline - start < 30:
        _GLOBAL["deadline"] = old_deadline
        a = normalize_sequence(a0)
        return a, score_normalized(a)

    _GLOBAL["deadline"] = main_deadline

    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = score_normalized(a)

    it = 0
    max_calls = 5

    try:
        while time.monotonic() < main_deadline - 30.0 and it < max_calls:
            h = lp_direction(a)
            if h is None:
                break

            improved = False
            for alpha in (0.2, 0.5, 0.8):
                cand = normalize_sequence((1.0 - alpha) * a + alpha * h)
                sc = score_normalized(cand)

                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best = cand.copy()
                    best_score = sc
                    a = cand
                    improved = True
                    break

            if not improved:
                break

            it += 1

    finally:
        _GLOBAL["deadline"] = old_deadline

    return best, best_score


def local_peak_search(a0, wall_time, rng, tag="", candidate_count=3, use_lp=False):
    old_deadline = _GLOBAL["deadline"]
    start = time.monotonic()
    local_deadline = min(old_deadline, start + float(wall_time))

    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    n = a.size

    if n == 0 or local_deadline - start <= 0.5:
        _GLOBAL["deadline"] = old_deadline
        if n == 0:
            return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64), float("inf")
        return a, score_normalized(a)

    _GLOBAL["deadline"] = local_deadline

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = score_normalized(a)

    eta = 0.8
    width = 0.05
    temp = 1e-5
    fail = 0
    it = 0
    kmax = max(2, min(16, n // 32))

    lp_used = 0
    lp_max = 3 if (use_lp and n <= 560 and wall_time > 250) else 0
    last_print = start - 30.0

    try:
        while True:
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now >= local_deadline - 0.2:
                break

            c = linear_convolution_same(a)
            if c.size == 0:
                break

            m = float(np.max(c))
            if not math.isfinite(m):
                break

            score = 2.0 * n * m

            if math.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-14:
                best = a.copy()
                best_score = score
                fail = 0

            if now - last_print > 20.0 or (it > 0 and it % 5000 == 0):
                print(
                    f"{tag}: it={it} cur={score:.6f} best={best_score:.6f} "
                    f"eta={eta:.3f} width={width:.3f} fail={fail}"
                )
                last_print = now

            active, weights = select_active_peaks(c, width, kmax, rng)
            grad = active_gradient(a, active, weights)
            gmax = float(np.max(grad))

            if math.isfinite(gmax) and gmax > 0.0:
                s_unit = grad / gmax

                etas = []
                for mult in (0.3, 0.6, 1.0, 1.6, 2.5):
                    e = eta * mult
                    if 0.02 < e < 10.0:
                        etas.append(e)

                if not etas:
                    etas = [0.5]

                etas = sorted({round(float(e), 8) for e in etas})[
                    : max(1, int(candidate_count))
                ]

                current_score = score
                chosen_cand = None
                chosen_score = current_score
                chosen_eta = eta

                for e in etas:
                    cand = exp_update(a, s_unit, e)
                    cm = float(np.max(linear_convolution_same(cand)))
                    sc = 2.0 * n * cm

                    if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < chosen_score:
                        chosen_score = sc
                        chosen_cand = cand
                        chosen_eta = e

                if chosen_cand is not None:
                    if metropolis_accept(current_score, chosen_score, temp, rng):
                        a = chosen_cand
                        score = chosen_score

                        if math.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-14:
                            best = a.copy()
                            best_score = score
                            fail = 0
                        elif chosen_score > current_score:
                            fail = min(fail + 1, 100000)

                        if chosen_eta < eta:
                            eta = max(0.05, eta * 0.85)
                        elif chosen_eta > eta:
                            eta = min(8.0, eta * 1.08)
                    else:
                        eta = max(0.05, eta * 0.85)
                        fail += 1
                else:
                    eta = max(0.05, eta * 0.85)
                    fail += 1
            else:
                fail += 1

            if fail > 0:
                if n <= 768:
                    kick_prob = 0.001 + min(0.15, 0.0004 * fail)
                else:
                    kick_prob = 0.0005 + min(0.10, 0.0002 * fail)
            else:
                kick_prob = 0.001

            if rng.random() < kick_prob:
                sigma = min(0.35, 0.02 + 0.01 * min(20, fail // 50))
                cand = random_perturb(a, rng, sigma, c)
                cm = float(np.max(linear_convolution_same(cand)))
                sc = 2.0 * n * cm

                if math.isfinite(sc) and metropolis_accept(score, sc, temp, rng):
                    a = cand
                    score = sc

                    if sc < best_score - 1e-14:
                        best = a.copy()
                        best_score = sc
                        fail = 0

            if (
                use_lp
                and lp_used < lp_max
                and fail > 600
                and (local_deadline - now) > 120.0
            ):
                h = lp_direction(a)
                lp_used += 1

                if h is not None:
                    improved = False

                    for alpha in (0.25, 0.5):
                        cand = normalize_sequence((1.0 - alpha) * a + alpha * h)
                        cm = float(np.max(linear_convolution_same(cand)))
                        sc = 2.0 * n * cm

                        if math.isfinite(sc) and metropolis_accept(score, sc, temp, rng):
                            a = cand
                            score = sc

                            if sc < best_score - 1e-14:
                                best = a.copy()
                                best_score = sc
                                fail = 0

                            improved = True
                            break

                    if not improved:
                        fail += 1

            if fail > 4000:
                sigma = 0.05 + 0.10 * rng.random()
                a = random_perturb(best, rng, sigma, None)
                score = score_normalized(a)
                fail = 0
                eta = 1.0
                width = 0.08

            if fail > 250:
                width = min(0.30, width * 1.02)
            else:
                width = max(0.02, width * 0.9995)

            temp = max(1e-8, temp * 0.9999)
            it += 1

    finally:
        _GLOBAL["deadline"] = old_deadline

    return best, best_score


def quick_optimize(a0, wall_time, rng, tag=""):
    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    n = a.size

    if n == 0 or wall_time <= 2.0:
        if n == 0:
            return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64), float("inf")
        return a, score_normalized(a)

    if n > 1024 or wall_time < 20.0:
        candidate_count = 2
    else:
        candidate_count = 3

    return local_peak_search(
        a,
        wall_time,
        rng,
        tag=tag,
        candidate_count=candidate_count,
        use_lp=False,
    )


def intensive_optimize(a0, wall_time, rng, tag=""):
    old_deadline = _GLOBAL["deadline"]
    start = time.monotonic()
    main_deadline = min(old_deadline, start + float(wall_time))

    if main_deadline - start <= 5.0:
        _GLOBAL["deadline"] = old_deadline
        a = normalize_sequence(a0)
        if a.size == 0:
            return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64), float("inf")
        return a, score_normalized(a)

    _GLOBAL["deadline"] = main_deadline

    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = score_normalized(a)
    n = best.size

    try:
        rem = max(0.0, main_deadline - time.monotonic())
        if rem > 20.0:
            t_local = min(rem - 10.0, max(10.0, 0.50 * wall_time))
            cc = 3 if n > 768 else 4
            a, sc = local_peak_search(
                a,
                t_local,
                rng,
                tag=tag + "_lp1",
                candidate_count=cc,
                use_lp=True,
            )

            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best = a.copy()
                best_score = sc

        rem = max(0.0, main_deadline - time.monotonic())
        if rem > 60.0 and n <= 1200:
            t_smooth = min(
                0.45 * rem,
                max(30.0, 0.25 * wall_time),
                600.0,
            )

            try:
                a, sc = smooth_minimize(a, t_smooth, tag=tag + "_smooth")
                _GLOBAL["deadline"] = main_deadline

                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best = a.copy()
                    best_score = sc

            except Exception:
                _GLOBAL["deadline"] = main_deadline

        rem = max(0.0, main_deadline - time.monotonic())
        if rem > 90.0 and n <= 700:
            t_lp = min(90.0, 0.25 * rem)

            try:
                a, sc = lp_polish(a, t_lp, tag=tag + "_lp")
                _GLOBAL["deadline"] = main_deadline

                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best = a.copy()
                    best_score = sc

            except Exception:
                _GLOBAL["deadline"] = main_deadline

        rem = max(0.0, main_deadline - time.monotonic())
        if rem > 20.0:
            a_start = best
            a, sc = local_peak_search(
                a_start,
                max(0.0, rem - 5.0),
                rng,
                tag=tag + "_lp2",
                candidate_count=3 if n > 768 else 4,
                use_lp=False,
            )

            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best = a.copy()
                best_score = sc

    finally:
        _GLOBAL["deadline"] = old_deadline

    if best is None:
        best = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)

    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    try:
        total_budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total_budget = 2400.0

    if not math.isfinite(total_budget) or total_budget <= 0.0:
        total_budget = 2400.0

    reserve = min(60.0, max(20.0, 0.025 * total_budget))
    main_deadline = t0 + max(60.0, total_budget - reserve)
    _GLOBAL["deadline"] = main_deadline

    try:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 42)
    except Exception:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(42)

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    provided = kwargs.get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if provided is None:
        provided = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)

    if provided is not None:
        try:
            a = normalize_sequence(provided)
            sc = score_normalized(a)
            print(f"Provided construction: n={a.size}, score={sc:.6f}")

            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = a.copy()

        except Exception:
            best_seq = None
            best_score = float("inf")

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = normalize_sequence(np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64))
        best_score = 2.0
        print("Fallback: uniform 256 sequence, score=2.000000")

    usable = max(60.0, total_budget - reserve)
    final_reserve = max(120.0, 0.30 * usable)
    explore_deadline = main_deadline - final_reserve

    # Phase 1: intensify the initial / provided candidate.
    if total_budget > 120.0:
        now = time.monotonic()
        p1_time = min(0.42 * usable, max(0.0, explore_deadline - now - 30.0))

        if p1_time > 30.0:
            print(f"\n=== Phase 1: initial intensive optimization, budget={p1_time:.1f}s ===")
            try:
                a, sc = intensive_optimize(best_seq, p1_time, rng, "init")
                if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_seq = a.copy()
                    print(f"Phase 1 new best: {best_score:.6f}, n={best_seq.size}")
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Phase 2: controlled exploration over lengths, initializations, global Fourier search.
    print("\n=== Phase 2: exploration ===")

    explore_candidates = []

    if provided is not None:
        for L in (384, 512, 768, 1024, 1536, 2048):
            try:
                if L <= 2048:
                    explore_candidates.append((f"resize{L}", L, resize_sequence(provided, L)))
            except Exception:
                pass

    for L in (128, 192, 256, 320, 384, 512, 768, 1024, 1536, 2048):
        try:
            explore_candidates.append((f"rand{L}", L, make_random_sequence(L, rng)))
        except Exception:
            pass

    for tag, L, a in explore_candidates:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= explore_deadline - 30.0:
            break
        if main_deadline - now <= 60.0:
            break

        if L <= 512:
            qt = 25.0
        elif L <= 1024:
            qt = 15.0
        else:
            qt = 8.0

        max_qt = explore_deadline - now - 5.0
        if max_qt < 3.0:
            continue

        qt = min(qt, max_qt)

        try:
            a2, sc = quick_optimize(a, qt, rng, tag)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = a2.copy()
                print(f"Exploration {tag}: new best {best_score:.6f}, n={best_seq.size}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Global low-dimensional Fourier search on a couple of lengths.
    for L in (256, 384, 512):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= explore_deadline - 60.0:
            break

        ft = min(60.0, explore_deadline - now - 30.0)
        if ft < 30.0:
            break

        try:
            a, sc = fourier_global_search(L, ft, rng)
            if a is not None and math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = a.copy()
                print(f"DE Fourier n={L}: new best {best_score:.6f}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Phase 3: final intensive polish on the best candidate found so far.
    now = time.monotonic()
    final_reserve_only = max(30.0, main_deadline - now - 20.0)

    print("\n=== Phase 3: final polished optimization ===")

    if (
        best_seq is not None
        and best_seq.size <= 1024
        and main_deadline - now > 200.0
    ):
        target_len = max(2 * int(best_seq.size), 512)
        if target_len <= 2048 and target_len != best_seq.size:
            t_up = min(80.0, 0.15 * final_reserve_only)
            if t_up > 20.0:
                try:
                    a_big = resize_sequence(best_seq, target_len)
                    a2, sc = quick_optimize(
                        a_big,
                        t_up,
                        rng,
                        f"upscale{target_len}",
                    )
                    if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_seq = a2.copy()
                        print(f"Upscale n={target_len}: new best {best_score:.6f}")
                except Exception:
                    pass

    now = time.monotonic()
    final_time = max(0.0, main_deadline - now - 20.0)

    if final_time > 30.0 and best_seq is not None:
        try:
            a, sc = intensive_optimize(best_seq, final_time, rng, "final")
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = a.copy()
                print(f"Final polish: new best {best_score:.6f}, n={best_seq.size}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_seq is None:
        final_arr = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        final_arr = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        final_arr = np.clip(final_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

        if final_arr.size == 0:
            final_arr = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)

        s = float(final_arr.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
            final_arr = final_arr + (0.01 - s) / max(1, final_arr.size)
            s = float(final_arr.sum())

        if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
            final_arr = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)

    final_list = [float(x) for x in final_arr]

    try:
        exact_score = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
        if not math.isfinite(exact_score):
            exact_score = score_normalized(final_arr)
        print(f"\nFINAL: n={len(final_list)}, internal={best_score:.6f}, evaluation={float(exact_score):.6f}")
    except Exception:
        try:
            exact_score = score_normalized(np.asarray(final_list, dtype=np.float64))
        except Exception:
            exact_score = float("inf")
        print(f"\nFINAL: n={len(final_list)}, internal={best_score:.6f}, internal-score={exact_score:.6f}")

    return final_list
```
